ARIMA - Chapter 215

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the arima object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 245. The moduli space has dimension at most 556 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on arima structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 245. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 556. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The arima operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 202. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 158 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to arima structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 202/158 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
